Title: Compendio storico di memorie cronologiche concernenti la religione e la morale della nazione armena suddita dell'Impero Ottomano; opera divisa in sei libri e presentata alla Sacra Congregazione di Propaganda; Tomo primo
Creator: Giovanni de Serpos ('il Marchese Giovanni de Serpos' on the title page)
Publisher: Venezia: nella stamperia di Carlo Palese, con pubblica approvazione
Publication Date: 1786
Rights: Public Domain (published 1786, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item's own rights statement reads 'NOT_IN_COPYRIGHT')
Language: ita
Source: Internet Archive item compendiostoric01serpgoog (https://archive.org/details/compendiostoric01serpgoog), the item's own compendiostoric01serpgoog_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: Oxford University; host metadata date: 1786. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: 65219f1ae4e50c95f1125c83fb697e12b73dbf4a191da25b3d1b1688c6f3113c.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; the host's front matter up to and including the line beginning 'at|http: //books' removed (46 lines); nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. Google scan of the Oxford copy; the Google notice at the head is removed. The title-page year is read as MDCCLXXXVI across the three volumes (the OCR garbles the last letters); the host gives 1786.
Content note: The first volume of a history of the religion and morals of the Armenian nation under Ottoman rule, written for Propaganda by an Armenian Catholic layman of Venice; the author's dedicatory letter to the Congregation opens it. The narrative begins from the conversion under Gregory the Illuminator.
